Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the holomorphic object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 14. The moduli space has dimension at most 397 with 2 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_14(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 14. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^397(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
